, we can see GFN-ROM is well able to capture the overall solution. Note that this performance is very good, particularly considering only 60 parameter realisations are seen for training, and the model learns a latent representation of size
L=3
. Interestingly, for the Graetz problem we see that errors are much higher for small values of
\mu_{2}
i.e. in the low diffusivity region, where the advection dominates. Moreover, few training samples are representing this regime, making it more difficult for GFN-ROM to learn how to model the region.

Nonetheless, the overall performance on GFN-ROM is extremely good on this benchmark, achieving over a 3x reduction in mean relative error compared to linear methods (see
Table 1
), and comparable performance to GCA-ROM.

(a)

Training on large mesh.

(b)

Training on medium mesh.

(c)

Training on small mesh.

(d)

Training on tiny mesh.

Figure 11
.
GFN-ROM solution fields for the Graetz problem when trained on (A) large mesh, (B) medium mesh, (C) small mesh, and (D) tiny mesh. The mesh points are shown by white pluses.

Moreover, we can further improve the performance of GFN-ROM by leveraging its resolution invariance. In fact, when training on a coarser representation, e.g. the medium mesh, the mean relative error is reduced to 0.88%, illustrating the new opportunities arising from GFN-ROM’s ability to perform super-resolution. Even when training on the coarsest (tiny) discretisation, the mean relative error increases only slightly by 0.26% to 1.28% despite this representing only 4% of the training data. In
Figure 11
, we show the predictions of the four different GFM-ROM models trained on the four different meshes for a parameter realisation
\bm{\mu}=(1.44,0.06)
not seen during training. The model predictions are qualitatively indistinguishable, showing that GFN-ROM is capable of extracting enough information even on cheaper computational data without incurring any penalty on performance. Even further generalisations are possible thanks to the possibility of training on mixed-fidelity data. GFN-ROM shows excellent generalisation properties and performance is minorly impacted when substituting high-fidelity data for lower-fidelity data, as seen in
Table 2
. For example, the performance even improves from 1.02% when training solely on the large mesh to 0.96% when substituting half of the large mesh dataset with cheaper data from the medium mesh (representing a 35% reduction in training data).

8.2.
Advection-dominated problem

Advection-dominated problems, due to the slow decay of the Kolmogorov
n
-width
[
36
,
73
]
, represent an important benchmarking case in the ROM context to compare linear and nonlinear reduction strategies. We consider a fixed domain
\Omega=[0,1]\times[0,1]\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, illustrated in
12(a)
with homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions. We generate four meshes summarised in Table
3
.

(a)

(b)

Figure 12
.
(A) The parameterised domain for the advection problem. (B) Mean relative errors for GFN-ROM trained on the large mesh for the advection problem. Squares indicate the parameter realisations seen during training.

The weak formulation of the advection problem reads as follows:
given
\bm{\mu}\in\mathbb{P}
, find
u(\bm{\mu})\in\mathbb{V}
s.t.

(33)

\displaystyle{\operatorname{D}(\mu_{1})}\int_{\Omega}\nabla u\cdot\nabla v\,d\Omega+\int_{\Omega}(\bm{\beta}\cdot\nabla u)v\,d\Omega=\int_{\Omega}v\,d\Omega,\quad\forall v\in\mathbb{V}=H^{1}_{0}(\Omega).

A total of 100 snapshots are generated for this problem on a uniform grid of
\mathbb{P}
, with 10 and 10 equispaced values for
\mu_{1}\in[0,6]
and
\mu_{2}\in[-1,1]
, respectively. The first parameter
\mu_{1}
determines the diffusion coefficient via
\operatorname{D}(\mu_{1})=10^{-\mu_{1}}
and the second parameter
\mu_{2}
determines the strength and sign of the transport
\bm{\beta}(\mu_{2})=(\mu_{2},\mu_{2})
.

As expected, POD-based techniques perform poorly due to their linear nature on this benchmark, with POD-G only achieving a mean relative error of 27.09% over the full dataset. GFN-ROM’s nonlinear nature allows it to achieve a far superior mean relative error of only 4.73% when training on the large mesh, which matches GCA-ROM’s performance. We show the relative errors over the parameter space in
12(b)
. We stress that this performance is possible even in the low data regime with only 30 training examples and a small latent representation of size
L=3
. Contrarily to the Graetz problem, it can be seen that poorer performances occur for lower Péclet numbers (
\operatorname{Pe}\approx\lvert\frac{\mu_{2}}{10^{-\mu_{1}}}\rvert
) compared to higher Péclet numbers i.e. the model performs better when transport dominates. This is likely due to the fact that the model training data is skewed towards examples where the advection in the opposite direction dominates, making it less adept at predicting solutions where diffusion has a stronger impact and the
\mu_{2}
is positive.

On this benchmark, GFN-ROM’s resolution invariant nature allows for training on cheaper meshes, and outperforming GCA-ROM, achieving a mean relative error of 4.48% when training on the medium mesh (69% reduction in training data). In
Figure 13
, we show the predictions of the GFN-ROM models trained on the four meshes for a parameter
\bm{\mu}=(3.33,-0.78)
not seen during training. Qualitatively, we can see that all models but tiny are capable of learning well-enough the solution dynamics. The tradeoff to consider is of course given by the performance of large models and the computational efficiency of the smaller ones. Similarly to the Graetz benchmark, we also observed good generalisation for the multifidelity cases, as shown in
Table 2
.

(a)

Training on large mesh.

(b)

Training on medium mesh.

(c)

Training on small mesh.

(d)

Training on tiny mesh.

Figure 13
.
GFN-ROM solution fields for the advection problem when trained on (A) large mesh, (B) medium mesh, (C) small mesh, and (D) tiny mesh. The mesh points are shown by white pluses.

8.3.
Stokes problem

We now consider a more complex benchmark in computational fluid dynamics, modelling a steady-state Stokes flow problem with high-dimensional physical/geometric parameter space and sparse sampling of it. This setting makes the problem particularly challenging to learn, and an important test case for the ability of a ROM to deal with the low-data regime. For simplicity, we consider the case of a viscosity of fixed value
1
, but we allow for a variable forcing term
{\mbox{\boldmath{$f$}}}=(0,\mu_{6})
, governed by a physical parameter
\mu_{6}
. We also consider a total of 6 geometrical parameters
\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}}=(\mu_{0},\mu_{1},\mu_{2},\mu_{3},\mu_{4},\mu_{5})
, to deform the domain depicted in
Figure 14
. We generate four meshes summarised in
Table 3
.

(0,\mu_{0}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3})
(\mu_{1},\mu_{0}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3})
(\mu_{1}+\mu_{4},\mu_{0}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{4}\tan(\mu_{5}))
(\mu_{1}+\mu_{4},\mu_{2}+\mu_{4}\tan(\mu_{5}))
(0,\mu_{0}+\mu_{2})
(0,\mu_{2})
(0,0)
(\mu_{1},0)
\Omega(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}})
\Gamma_{\text{w}}
\Gamma_{\text{N}}
\Gamma_{\text{N}}
\Gamma_{\text{w}}
\Gamma_{\text{w}}
\Gamma_{\text{in}}

Figure 14
.
The domain for the Stokes problem.

As boundary conditions, the inlet
\Gamma_{\text{in}}
is given a Dirichlet boundary condition of
{\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{in}=[4(y-1)(2-y),0]
, the walls
\Gamma_{\text{w}}
are given zero Dirichlet boundary conditions and the outlet
\Gamma_{\text{N}}
is left as a homogeneous Neumann boundary condition. A weak formulation for this problem reads: for a given parameter
\bm{\mu}\in\mathbb{P}
, find
\bm{u}(\bm{\mu})\in\mathbb{U}
,
p\in\mathbb{M}
s.t.

(34)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\int_{\Omega}\nabla\bm{u}:\nabla\bm{v}\,d\Omega-\int_{\Omega}p\nabla\cdot\bm{v}\,d\Omega=\int_{\Omega}\bm{f}\cdot\bm{v}\,d\Omega,\quad\forall\bm{v}\in\mathbb{V},\\
\int_{\Omega}q\nabla\cdot\bm{u}\,d\Omega=0,\quad\forall q\in\mathbb{M},\end{cases}

where the parameter dependent function spaces for the velocity are defined as
\mathbb{V}(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}})=\{{\mbox{\boldmath{$v$}}}\in(H^{1}(\Omega(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}}))^{2}:{\mbox{\boldmath{$v$}}}|_{\Gamma_{w}\cup\Gamma_{in}}=\bm{0}\}
and
\mathbb{U}(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}})=\{{\mbox{\boldmath{$v$}}}\in(H^{1}(\Omega(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}})))^{2}:{\mbox{\boldmath{$v$}}}|_{\Gamma_{w}}=\bm{0},{\mbox{\boldmath{$v$}}}|_{\Gamma_{in}}={\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{in}\}
, and the function space for the pressure is defined as
\mathbb{M}(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}})=L^{2}(\Omega(\bm{\mu}_{\text{geo}}))
. The problem uses a mixed finite element discretisation with the velocity and pressure as solution variables, meaning that the inf-sup condition is necessary for the well posedness of this problem and as a result the supremiser operator
T^{\mu}:\mathbb{M}\rightarrow\mathbb{V}
is used (see
[
6
]
for more details).

We consider a sparse sampling with solutions computed only for the following parameter choices:
\mu_{0},\mu_{1},\mu_{2},\mu_{3},\mu_{4}\in\{0.5,1.5\},\mu_{5}\in\{-\frac{\pi}{6},\frac{\pi}{6}\},\mu_{6}\in\{-10,-8,-6,-4,-2,0,2,4,6,8,10\}
, leading to a total of 704 high-fidelity solutions, and the field of interest is the magnitude of the the velocity
|{\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}|
.

Figure 15
.
Histogram of the mean relative error for GFN-ROM trained on the large mesh for the Stokes problem.

Training on the large mesh, GFN-ROM achieves a mean relative error of 4.24%, outperforming GCA-ROM. We stress that this performance is possible whilst remaining in the low data regime, undersampling the parameter space. Despite this, the model is well able to capture the solution. As shown in
Figure 15
, model performance is found to be very similar on average for the possible geometrical parameters, while, as expected, the results are more sensitive to the physical parameter
\mu_{6}
, with slightly worse approximation for the case
\mu_{6}=0
where the forcing term vanishes.

GFN-ROM shows even further performance improvements by training on cheaper computational meshes, achieving a mean relative error of 4.12% for the medium mesh (representing a 68% reduction in the number of nodes). Even when training on the coarsest discretisation with just 4% of the data, performance only increases to 5.55% and is still able to capture the behaviour with respect to the physical and geometric parameters not seen during training, respectively in
Figure 16
and
Figure 17
.

Figure 16
.
Super-resolution via GFN-ROM when training on the tiny mesh and evaluating on large mesh for variations in the physical parameter
\mu_{6}
showcasing downward, none and upward forcings.

Figure 17
.
Super-resolution via GFN-ROM when training on the tiny mesh and evaluating on large mesh for variations in the geometrical parameters.

Once again, the complex parametric variability and the scarce amount of information (training on 211 parameter realisations for the 7-dimensional parameter space) do not compromise the generalisation capability of the methodology.

We also see excellent results for multifidelity training. In general, performances are only minorly affected when substituting half of the data for cheaper data, underlining the possibility of training on cheaper data whilst retaining excellent performance. It is even possible to achieve a better performance of 3.63% by replacing 50% of data on the large mesh with cheaper data on the medium mesh (representing a 34% reduction in training data), as detailed in
Table 2
.

9.
Conclusion

In this work, we presented a novel graph-based resolution-invariant ROM, as a powerful tool to investigate multifidelity applications in the MOR context. We showed that it is possible to apply feedforward networks for graphical data by means of the graph feedforward network, allowing for the extension of many single-fidelity architectures to the multifidelity setting. Furthermore, we showed that this extension comes with provable guarantees on the performance in terms of error bounds.

Beyond such theoretical guarantees, we have also demonstrated that our architecture performs well in practice on three challenging benchmarks involving parametrised domains, advection-dominated problems and high-dimensional parameter spaces. Across all benchmarks, GFN-ROM achieved satisfactory performances, especially considering we worked within a low-data regime. Indeed, like GCA-ROM, our architecture has been trained on only 30% of the dataset, and has been exploited to learn a small latent representation.
GFN-ROM achieved comparable or better performance than GCA-ROM whilst being more flexible, interpretable and lightweight, and applicable for multifidelity data.

We showed that GFN-ROM can perform super-resolution, and demonstrated that within the proposed architecture, it is possible to substitute high-fidelity data for lower-fidelity data without incurring performance penalties. In practice, we showed that often performance can even be improved when training on cheaper computational data. Our work thus highlighted the importance of multifidelity ROMs, showing that computational
savings can be made by training on smaller meshes without deterioration in performance.

Future work will investigate further possibilities with graph feedforward networks, including tackling time-dependent problems, problems with spatially dependent parameters, enforcing more smoothness in GFN-ROM predictions, forcing greater dependence on local connections and automating finding the best training mesh (adapting the number of model parameters during training). We also plan to investigate multimodal modelling by using information from the Fourier domain, and the generalisation of convolutional neural operators to unstructured grids.

Appendix A
Proofs

A.1.
Extension to multiple layers

For ease of notation we define each of the outputs of the
\operatorname{GFN}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}
function as

\displaystyle\operatorname{GFN}_{W^{e}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e}),\penalty\ \operatorname{GFN}_{W^{d}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d}),\penalty\ \operatorname{GFN}_{b^{d}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$b$}}}^{d})=\operatorname{GFN}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e},{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d},{\mbox{\boldmath{$b$}}}^{d}).

The single-layer GFN autoencoder given in
Equation 6
and
Equation 7
can then be rewritten as

\displaystyle\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma\left(\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{n}}\operatorname{GFN}_{W^{e}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e})_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}u_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}+b^{e}_{i}\right),\qquad\forall i=1,\cdots,L,

\displaystyle\operatorname{dec}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$z$}}})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{L}\operatorname{GFN}_{W^{d}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}z_{j}+b^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\qquad\forall i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1,\cdots,N_{n}.

This can be extended to
Q+1
hidden layers as

\displaystyle\operatorname{enc}_{0}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}})_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma\left(\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{n}}\operatorname{GFN}_{W^{e}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e})_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}x_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}+b^{e}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\forall{i=1,\cdots,L_{1}},

\displaystyle\operatorname{enc}_{P\cdots 1}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}})_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma\left(\sum_{l_{P}=1}^{L_{P}}W^{(P)}_{i,l_{P}}\sigma\left(\cdots\sigma\left(\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{L_{1}}W^{(1)}_{l_{2},l_{1}}x_{l_{1}}+b^{(1)}_{l_{2}}\right)+\cdots\right)+b^{(P)}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\forall{i=1,\cdots,L_{P+1}},

\displaystyle\operatorname{dec}_{Q\cdots P+1}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}})_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma\left(\sum_{l_{Q}=1}^{L_{Q}}W^{(Q)}_{i,l_{Q}}\sigma\left(\cdots\sigma\left(\sum_{l_{P+1}=1}^{L_{P+1}}W^{(P+1)}_{l_{P+2},l_{P+1}}x_{l_{P+1}}+b^{(P+1)}_{l_{P+2}}\right)+\cdots\right)+b^{(Q)}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\forall{i=1,\cdots,L_{Q+1}},

\displaystyle\operatorname{dec}_{Q+1}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{L_{Q+1}}\operatorname{GFN}_{W^{d}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}x_{j}+\operatorname{GFN}_{b^{d}}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$b$}}}^{d})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},

\displaystyle\forall i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1,\cdots,N_{n},

where
0\leq P\leq Q
,
\mathcal{M}_{n}
is a new mesh,
L_{i}
represents the output sizes for the
(i+1)
-th network layer with
L_{P+1}=L
and
L_{Q+2}=N_{n}
,
\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}})_{i}=\operatorname{enc}_{P\cdots 1}(\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}_{0}({\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}}))_{i},\forall i=1,\cdots,L
is the encoded latent representation of an input
{\mbox{\boldmath{$x$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{N_{n}}
on mesh
\mathcal{M}_{n}
and
\operatorname{dec}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$z$}}})_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}=\operatorname{dec}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}_{Q+1}(\operatorname{dec}_{Q\cdots P+1}({\mbox{\boldmath{$z$}}}))_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\forall i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1,\cdots,N_{n}
is the reconstructed solution on
\mathcal{M}_{n}
from a given latent representation
{\mbox{\boldmath{$z$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{L}
.

A.2.
Simplified expansive equations

We consider the GFN transforms
\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
and show that they can be simplified under the expansive condition given in
Equation 9
.

First of all, we show that the condition implies

\displaystyle\forall i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},\quad\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\},

which is a set containing a unique element
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
defined as
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
.

Proof:

We have to show that
\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\cup\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}
. If
\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\emptyset
, this is trivially the case. Therefore, in the following we consider only the case where it is not.

Trivially,
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1
since a node
i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
can only have one nearest neighbour. In fact, additionally we can show it is also the case that
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1
, which can be proven by contradiction. Suppose that
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=R\neq 1
. This means that there exist multiple distinct nodes
\left\{k^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\right\}_{r=1}^{R}
, each satisfying
k^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
. Via the expansive condition in
Equation 9
, it follows that each of these nodes must also satisfy
k^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
. This is not possible since
i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
can only have a single nearest neighbour, meaning therefore
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1
.

We note that the single node in the set
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|
must also satisfy
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
due to
Equation 9
. Therefore, it is in fact the exact same node as the node in the set
|\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|
, thus concluding the proof.

Secondly, we show that for a given
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}

\displaystyle\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert=\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert.

Proof:

Due to
Equation 9
, it is the case that
\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\subseteq\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}
, concluding the proof.

The updates can therefore be simplified to

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{e}_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|}{W}^{e}_{ik_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{W}^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},

\displaystyle\tilde{b}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{b}^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},

which therefore concludes the proof.

A.3.
Simplified agglomerative equations

We consider the GFN transforms
\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
and show that they can be simplified under the agglomerative condition given in
Equation 11
.

First of all, we show that the condition implies

\displaystyle\forall j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},\quad\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}.

Proof:

Due to
Equation 11
, it is the case that
\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\subseteq\{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}
, concluding the proof.

Secondly, we show that for a given
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}

\displaystyle\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert=1.

Proof:

We want to show that

|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t.
