}k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\cup\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1.

If
\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}=\emptyset
, this is trivially the case since
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
only has one nearest neighbour i.e.
\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert=1
. Therefore, in the following we consider only the case where it is not.

With this, we can show it is also the case that
|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1
, which can be proven by contradiction. Suppose that
|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=R\neq 1
. This means that there exist multiple distinct nodes
\left\{h^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\right\}_{r=1}^{R}
, each satisfying
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
. Via the agglomerative condition in
Equation 11
, it follows that each of these nodes must also satisfy
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h^{r}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
. This is not possible since
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
can only have a single nearest neighbour, meaning therefore
|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|=1
.

We note that the single node in the set
|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|
must also satisfy
k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}
due to
Equation 11
. Therefore, it is in fact the exact same node as the node in the set
|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|
, thus concluding the proof.

The updates can therefore be simplified to

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{e}_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}W^{e}_{ik_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}W^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j},

\displaystyle\tilde{b}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rightarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}b^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},

which therefore concludes the proof.

A.4.
Self-consistency

We consider a sequence of two transforms, firstly an expansive transform
\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
, and then secondly a transform
\mathcal{M}_{n}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}
. Note that if
\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
is expansive, then it must follow by definition that
\mathcal{M}_{n}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}
is agglomerative.
The first transform
\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
is expansive. Following
Equation 9
, this gives the first update as

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{e}_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}|}{W}^{e}_{ik_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{W}^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}},

\displaystyle\hat{b}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{b}^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},\qquad\text{ where}\quad k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}.

The second transform
\mathcal{M}_{o}\leftarrow\mathcal{M}_{n}
is agglomerative (note that the transform is
not

\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}
). Following
Equation 11
, this gives the second update as

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{e}_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\hat{W}^{e}_{ik_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}},

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow \displaystyle\tilde{b}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ }i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow Subbing everything in gives

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{e}_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\forall }l_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\leftarrow where}\quad A.5.

can be rewritten as

A.6.
Super- and sub-resolution error bound for GFN-ROM

We seek a bound on the following quantity

\displaystyle\left|u(x_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})-\operatorname{dec}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}(\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu}))_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\right|,\forall i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}.

We can rewrite the expression and bound it as

\displaystyle\left|u(x_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})-\operatorname{dec}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}(\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu}))_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|u(x_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})-\sum_{j=1}^{L}\tilde{W}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu})_{j}+\tilde{b}^{d}_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\right|,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}\left(\left|u(x_{i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})-u(x_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M} \displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}\left(\delta+\tau\right),

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta+\tau.

This concludes the proof. For the multiple layer case, the result is also the same.

A.7.
Super- and sub-resolution error bound for GFN-ROM mapper

We seek a bound on

(35)

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}\right|,\forall i.

We can rewrite the expression as

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}+\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{map}(\bm{\mu})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}\right|+\left|\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}\right|,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha+\left|\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{o}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}-\operatorname{enc}^{\mathcal{M}_{o}\to\mathcal{M}_{n}}({\mbox{\boldmath{$u$}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}})_{i}\right|,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha+\left|\sigma\left(\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}u(x_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})+b^{e}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left(\sum_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{n}}\tilde{W}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}i}^{e}u(x_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})+{\tilde{b}^{e}}_{i}\right)\right|.

We assume a Lipschitz condition with Lipschitz constant
C
for the activation function
\sigma
, which allows us to continue bounding as

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha+C\left|\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}u(x_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})+b^{e}_{i}-\left(\sum_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0 \displaystyle\alpha+C\left|\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}\frac{W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}}{\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\penalty\ {\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}\penalty\ h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert}\sum_{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\delta\right|,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha+\delta C\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}|W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}|.

To obtain a bound independent of
i
, one can bound as

\displaystyle\leq\alpha+\delta C||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e}||_{\infty}.

This concludes the proof. For the multiple layer case, the result is

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha+\delta C^{P+1}\sum_{l_{P}=1}^{L_{P}}|W^{(P)}_{i,l_{P}}|\sum_{l_{P-1}=1}^{L_{P-1}}|W^{(P-1)}_{l_{P},l_{P-1}}|\cdots\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{L_{1}}|W^{(1)}_{l_{2},l_{1}}|\penalty\ \sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}|W_{l_{1},j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}|,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha+\delta C^{P+1}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e}||_{\infty}\prod_{p=1}^{P}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{(p)}||_{\infty}.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\left|\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}\frac{W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}}{\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\penalty\ {\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}\penalty\ k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\left(u(x_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}})-u(x_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raise
