\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\left|\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}\frac{W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}}{\lvert\{h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t. }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{827776}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{785437}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{888616}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\penalty\ {\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{282168}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{327681}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}\penalty\ h_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.25435pt}{\resizebox{874305}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31706pt}{\resizebox{832916}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.735pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.31705pt}{\resizebox{955087}{393216}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\}\rvert}\sum_{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}\delta\right|,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta C\sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}|W_{ij_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}|.

Substituting these expressions into the original bound, we obtain

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta+\beta+\delta C\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}\sum_{j=1}^{L}\left|W^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}\right|\sum_{m_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}\left|W_{jm_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}^{e}\right|.

To obtain a bound independent of
i
, one can bound as

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta+\beta+\delta C||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d}||_{\infty}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e}||_{\infty}.

This concludes the proof. For the multiple layer case, the result is

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta+\delta+\delta C^{Q+1}\underset{\forall k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\text{ s.t }k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}{\leftarrow}\!{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{197518}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{138262}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{261235}{98759}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$$/$$}}}}}}\!{\rightarrow}i_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{659088}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{570935}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{658648}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{n}\mathstrut$}}}}}}}{\operatorname{mean}}\sum_{j=1}^{L_{Q+1}}|W^{d}_{k_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}j}|\sum_{l_{Q}=1}^{L_{Q}}|W^{(Q)}_{j,l_{Q}}|\cdots\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{L_{1}}|W^{(1)}_{l_{2},l_{1}}|\penalty\ \sum_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}=1}^{N_{o}}|W_{j_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.88266pt}{\resizebox{627905}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.75pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{539283}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.525pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87804pt}{\resizebox{614336}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.375pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{M}_{o}\mathstrut$}}}}}}l_{1}}^{e}|,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta+\delta+\delta C^{Q+1}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{d}||_{\infty}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{e}||_{\infty}\prod_{p=1}^{Q}||{\mbox{\boldmath{$W$}}}^{(p)}||_{\infty}.
